# Skills · S · part 290

820 entries, stadium-tester to stage-review. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **stadium-tester**: Verify a Stadium feature or fix by driving a real headless Chromium against the Vercel preview or local dev server. Use during /ship-issue's verify step, or any time you need to walk a UI through the test scenarios listed on a GitHub issue. Reads scenarios in the form "On &lt;route&gt;, &lt;action&gt; → &lt;expected state&gt;" and produces a pass/fail markdown report. ([StadiumDevs/stadium](https://github.com/StadiumDevs/stadium/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/stadium-tester) · ★1)
- **stadt-wien-figma-prototyper**: Create or update editable Figma prototypes and blueprints for Stadt Wien (wien.gv.at) using the Wiener Melange component kit and live theme; not for other sites or production code. ([dariob13/Figma-Prototyper](https://github.com/dariob13/Figma-Prototyper/tree/HEAD/skills/stadt-wien-figma-prototyper))
- **stadt-wien-html-prototyper**: Create editable HTML prototypes for Stadt Wien (wien.gv.at) pages using Paper or DOOP, sourced from the official pattern library at handbuch.wien.gv.at. Uses real component HTML code and live styling guidelines. ([dariob13/Figma-Prototyper](https://github.com/dariob13/Figma-Prototyper/tree/HEAD/skills/stadt-wien-html-prototyper))

- **stage-gate**: 当研究流程的任一阶段产出物完成、需要用户审批后推进时使用；通常由 research-lifecycle 在每个阶段末尾自动调用，用户也可以直接说"看看当前阶段状态""审批一下""approve""revise""reject""阶段门禁""stage gate"触发。本技能负责：把阶段产出物落盘、生成 stage-report.md（本阶段做了什么/关键结果/风险与疑虑/建议）、更新 output/&lt;project&gt;/stage.yaml，然后停止等待用户三选一——approve 进入下一阶段、revise 携带意见重跑本阶段（原产物归档不覆盖）、reject 终止并写结题说明。同义触发场景：阶段确认、继续下一步、打回重做、终止项目、恢复进度、上次进行到哪了。 ([MiniMax-AI/MiniMax-Code-Plugins](https://github.com/MiniMax-AI/MiniMax-Code-Plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/Hylouis233/openscience/skills/stage-gate) · ★25 · 3 copies)
